# CTCAE v5.0 to v6.0 Mapping: Gastrointestinal disorders

Verbatim extract of the sheet "CTCAE v5.0 to v6.0 Mapping" of the official CTCAE v6.0 workbook (https://dctd.cancer.gov/research/ctep-trials/trial-development/ctcae-v6.0.xlsx), rows whose "CTCAE v5.0 MedDRA 20.1 SOC" is "Gastrointestinal disorders". Retrieved 2026-11-08. The official version prevails. Terms of use, source details and conversion notes: ../ctcae-v6-vs-v5-changes.md.

Rows: 643; CTCAE v5.0 terms: 122.

### Gastroesophageal reflux disease

CTCAE v5.0 MedDRA 20.1 LLT Code: 10066874

- **CTCAE v5.0 Grade/Definition/Navigational Note:** Definition | **CTCAE v5.0 Description:** A disorder characterized by reflux of the gastric and/or duodenal contents into the distal esophagus.
